    } else {
      if (this.path !== "") {
        const l = z1.extractUrlBase(e);
        s = z1.resolveURL(l, this.path);
      } else {
        s = z1.extractUrlBase(e);
      }
    }
    this.manager.itemStart(e);
    const o = function (l) {
      i ? i(l) : console.error(l);
      r.manager.itemError(e);
      r.manager.itemEnd(e);
    };
    const a = new Bf(this.manager);
    a.setPath(this.path);
    a.setResponseType("arraybuffer");
    a.setRequestHeader(this.requestHeader);
    a.setWithCredentials(this.withCredentials);
    a.load(e, function (l) {
      try {
        r.parse(l, s, function (u) {
          t(u);
          r.manager.itemEnd(e);
        }, o);
      } catch (u) {
        o(u);
      }
    }, n, o);
  }
  setDRACOLoader(e) {
    this.dracoLoader = e;
    return this;
  }
  setDDSLoader() {
    throw new Error('THREE.GLTFLoader: "MSFT_texture_dds" no longer supported. Please update to "KHR_texture_basisu".');
  }
  setKTX2Loader(e) {
    this.ktx2Loader = e;
    return this;
  }
  setMeshoptDecoder(e) {
    this.meshoptDecoder = e;
    return this;
  }
  register(e) {
    this.pluginCallbacks.indexOf(e) === -1 && this.pluginCallbacks.push(e);
    return this;
  }
  unregister(e) {
    this.pluginCallbacks.indexOf(e) !== -1 && this.pluginCallbacks.splice(this.pluginCallbacks.indexOf(e), 1);
    return this;
  }
  parse(e, t, n, i) {
    let r;
    const s = {};
    const o = {};
    if (typeof e == "string") {
      r = JSON.parse(e);
    } else {
      if (e instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
        if (FR(new Uint8Array(e.slice(0, 4))) === One) {
          try {
            s[ss.KHR_BINARY_GLTF] = new PTe(e);
          } catch (l) {
            i && i(l);
            return;
          }
          r = JSON.parse(s[ss.KHR_BINARY_GLTF].content);
        } else {
          r = JSON.parse(FR(new Uint8Array(e)));
        }
      } else {
        r = e;
      }
    }
    if (r.asset === void 0 || r.asset.version[0] < 2) {
      i && i(new Error("THREE.GLTFLoader: Unsupported asset. glTF versions >=2.0 are supported."));
      return;
    }
    const a = new KTe(r, {
      path: t || this.resourcePath || "",
      crossOrigin: this.crossOrigin,
      requestHeader: this.requestHeader,
      manager: this.manager,
      ktx2Loader: this.ktx2Loader,
      meshoptDecoder: this.meshoptDecoder
    });
    a.fileLoader.setRequestHeader(this.requestHeader);
    for (let l = 0; l < this.pluginCallbacks.length; l++) {
      const u = this.pluginCallbacks[l](a);
      u.name || console.error("THREE.GLTFLoader: Invalid plugin found: missing name");
      o[u.name] = u;
      s[u.name] = !0;
    }
    if (r.extensionsUsed) {
      for (let l = 0; l < r.extensionsUsed.length; ++l) {
        const u = r.extensionsUsed[l];
        const d = r.extensionsRequired || [];
        switch (u) {
          case ss.KHR_MATERIALS_UNLIT:
            s[u] = new mTe();
            break;
          case ss.KHR_DRACO_MESH_COMPRESSION:
            s[u] = new LTe(r, this.dracoLoader);
            break;
          case ss.KHR_TEXTURE_TRANSFORM:
            s[u] = new NTe();
            break;
          case ss.KHR_MESH_QUANTIZATION:
            s[u] = new OTe();
            break;
          default:
            d.indexOf(u) >= 0 && o[u] === void 0 && console.warn('THREE.GLTFLoader: Unknown extension "' + u + '".');
        }
      }
    }
    a.setExtensions(s);
    a.setPlugins(o);
    a.parse(n, i);
  }
  parseAsync(e, t) {
    const n = this;
    return new Promise(function (i, r) {
      n.parse(e, t, i, r);
    });
  }
};
function gTe() {
  let e = {};
  return {
    get: function (t) {
      return e[t];
    },
    add: function (t, n) {
      e[t] = n;
    },
    remove: function (t) {
      delete e[t];
    },
    removeAll: function () {
      e = {};
    }
  };
}

var ATe = class {
  constructor(e) {
    this.parser = e;
    this.name = ss.KHR_LIGHTS_PUNCTUAL;
    this.cache = {
      refs: {},
      uses: {}
    };
  }
  _markDefs() {
    const e = this.parser;
    const t = this.parser.json.nodes || [];
    for (let n = 0, i = t.length; n < i; n++) {
      const r = t[n];
      r.extensions && r.extensions[this.name] && r.extensions[this.name].light !== void 0 && e._addNodeRef(this.cache, r.extensions[this.name].light);
    }
  }
  _loadLight(e) {
    const t = this.parser;
    const n = "light:" + e;
    let i = t.cache.get(n);
    if (i) {
      return i;
    }
    const r = t.json;
    const s = ((r.extensions && r.extensions[this.name] || {}).lights || [])[e];
    let o;
    const a = new Bn(16777215);
    s.color !== void 0 && a.setRGB(s.color[0], s.color[1], s.color[2], $v);
    const l = s.range !== void 0 ? s.range : 0;
    switch (s.type) {
      case "directional":
        o = new HF(a);
        o.target.position.set(0, 0, -1);
        o.add(o.target);
        break;
      case "point":
        o = new Ok(a);
        o.distance = l;
        break;
      case "spot":
        o = new QF(a);
        o.distance = l;
        s.spot = s.spot || {};
        s.spot.innerConeAngle = s.spot.innerConeAngle !== void 0 ? s.spot.innerConeAngle : 0;
        s.spot.outerConeAngle = s.spot.outerConeAngle !== void 0 ? s.spot.outerConeAngle : Math.PI / 4;
        o.angle = s.spot.outerConeAngle;
        o.penumbra = 1 - s.spot.innerConeAngle / s.spot.outerConeAngle;
        o.target.position.set(0, 0, -1);
        o.add(o.target);
        break;
      default:
        throw new Error("THREE.GLTFLoader: Unexpected light type: " + s.type);
    }
    o.position.set(0, 0, 0);
    o.decay = 2;
    Pv(o, s);
    s.intensity !== void 0 && (o.intensity = s.intensity);
    o.name = t.createUniqueName(s.name || "light_" + e);
    i = Promise.resolve(o);
    t.cache.add(n, i);
    return i;
  }
  getDependency(e, t) {
    if (e === "light") {
      return this._loadLight(t);
    }
  }
  createNodeAttachment(e) {
    const t = this;
    const n = this.parser;
    const i = n.json.nodes[e];
    const r = (i.extensions && i.extensions[this.name] || {}).light;
    return r === void 0 ? null : this._loadLight(r).then(function (s) {
      return n._getNodeRef(t.cache, r, s);
    });
  }
};

var DTe = class {

var Une = class extends DC {
  constructor(e, t, n, i) {
    super(e, t, n, i);
  }
  copySampleValue_(e) {
    const t = this.resultBuffer;
    const n = this.sampleValues;
    const i = this.valueSize;
    const r = e * i * 3 + i;
    for (let s = 0; s !== i; s++) {
      t[s] = n[r + s];
    }
    return t;
  }
  interpolate_(e, t, n, i) {
    const r = this.resultBuffer;
    const s = this.sampleValues;
    const o = this.valueSize;
    const a = o * 2;
    const l = o * 3;
    const u = i - t;
    const d = (n - t) / u;
    const h = d * d;
    const p = h * d;
    const A = e * l;
    const y = A - l;
    const E = -2 * p + 3 * h;
    const b = p - h;
    const w = 1 - E;
    const T = b - h + d;
    for (let x = 0; x !== o; x++) {
      const R = s[y + x + o];
      const D = s[y + x + a] * u;
      const O = s[A + x + o];
      const z = s[A + x] * u;
      r[x] = w * R + T * D + E * O + b * z;
    }
    return r;
  }
};

function zTe(e, t, n) {

      break;
    }
  }

    return Promise.resolve(e);
  }

  const l = [];

    const h = t[u];

      o.push(p);
    }

  });
}

      const A = i.normalized === !0;
      let y;
      let E;
      if (p && p !== d) {
        const b = Math.floor(h / p);
        const w = "InterleavedBuffer:" + i.bufferView + ":" + i.componentType + ":" + b + ":" + i.count;
        let T = t.cache.get(w);
        T || (y = new l(o, b * p, i.count * p / u), T = new kT(y, p / u), t.cache.add(w, T));
        E = new yw(T, a, h % p / u, A);
      } else {
        o === null ? y = new l(i.count * a) : y = new l(o, h, i.count * a);
        E = new Jr(y, a, A);
      }
      if (i.sparse !== void 0) {
        const b = A6.SCALAR;
        const w = LS[i.sparse.indices.componentType];
        const T = i.sparse.indices.byteOffset || 0;
        const x = i.sparse.values.byteOffset || 0;
        const R = new w(s[1], T, i.sparse.count * b);
        const D = new l(s[2], x, i.sparse.count * a);
        o !== null && (E = new Jr(E.array.slice(), E.itemSize, E.normalized));
        for (let O = 0, z = R.length; O < z; O++) {
          const Q = R[O];
          E.setX(Q, D[O * a]);
          a >= 2 && E.setY(Q, D[O * a + 1]);
          a >= 3 && E.setZ(Q, D[O * a + 2]);
          a >= 4 && E.setW(Q, D[O * a + 3]);
          if (a >= 5) {
            throw new Error("THREE.GLTFLoader: Unsupported itemSize in sparse BufferAttribute.");
          }
        }
      }
      return E;
    });
  }
  loadTexture(e) {
    const t = this.json;
    const n = this.options;
    const i = t.textures[e].source;
    const r = t.images[i];
    let s = this.textureLoader;
    if (r.uri) {
      const o = n.manager.getHandler(r.uri);
      o !== null && (s = o);
    }
    return this.loadTextureImage(e, i, s);
  }
  loadTextureImage(e, t, n) {
    const i = this;
    const r = this.json;
    const s = r.textures[e];
    const o = r.images[t];
    const a = (o.uri || o.bufferView) + ":" + s.sampler;
    if (this.textureCache[a]) {
      return this.textureCache[a];
    }
    const l = this.loadImageSource(t, n).then(function (u) {
      u.flipY = !1;
      u.name = s.name || o.name || "";
      u.name === "" && typeof o.uri == "string" && o.uri.startsWith("data:image/") === !1 && (u.name = o.uri);
      const d = (r.samplers || {})[s.sampler] || {};
      u.magFilter = _W[d.magFilter] || 1006;
      u.minFilter = _W[d.minFilter] || 1008;
      u.wrapS = xW[d.wrapS] || 1e3;
      u.wrapT = xW[d.wrapT] || 1e3;
      i.associations.set(u, {
        textures: e
      });
      return u;
    }).catch(function () {
      return null;
    });
    this.textureCache[a] = l;
    return l;
  }
  loadImageSource(e, t) {
    const n = this;
    const i = this.json;
    const r = this.options;
    if (this.sourceCache[e] !== void 0) {
      return this.sourceCache[e].then(d => d.clone());
    }
    const s = i.images[e];
    const o = self.URL || self.webkitURL;
    let a = s.uri || "";
    let l = !1;
    if (s.bufferView !== void 0) {
      a = n.getDependency("bufferView", s.bufferView).then(function (d) {
        l = !0;
        const h = new Blob([d], {
          type: s.mimeType
        });
        a = o.createObjectURL(h);
        return a;
      });
    } else {
      if (s.uri === void 0) {
        throw new Error("THREE.GLTFLoader: Image " + e + " is missing URI and bufferView");
      }
    }
    const u = Promise.resolve(a).then(function (d) {
      return new Promise(function (h, p) {
        let A = h;
        t.isImageBitmapLoader === !0 && (A = function (y) {
          const E = new wl(y);
          E.needsUpdate = !0;
          h(E);
        });
        t.load(z1.resolveURL(d, r.path), A, void 0, p);
      });
    }).then(function (d) {
      l === !0 && o.revokeObjectURL(a);
      Pv(d, s);
      d.userData.mimeType = s.mimeType || VTe(s.uri);
      return d;
    }).catch(function (d) {
      console.error("THREE.GLTFLoader: Couldn't load texture", a);
      throw d;
    });
    this.sourceCache[e] = u;
    return u;
  }
  assignTexture(e, t, n, i) {
    const r = this;
    return this.getDependency("texture", n.index).then(function (s) {
      if (!s) {
        return null;
      }
      n.texCoord !== void 0 && n.texCoord > 0 && (s = s.clone(), s.channel = n.texCoord);
      if (r.extensions[ss.KHR_TEXTURE_TRANSFORM]) {
        const o = n.extensions !== void 0 ? n.extensions[ss.KHR_TEXTURE_TRANSFORM] : void 0;
        if (o) {
          const a = r.associations.get(s);
          s = r.extensions[ss.KHR_TEXTURE_TRANSFORM].extendTexture(s, o);
          r.associations.set(s, a);
        }
      }
      i !== void 0 && (typeof i == "number" && (i = i === wW ? Gb : $v), "colorSpace" in s ? s.colorSpace = i : s.encoding = i === Gb ? wW : pTe);
      e[t] = s;
      return s;
    });
  }
  assignFinalMaterial(e) {
    const t = e.geometry;
    let n = e.material;
    const i = t.attributes.tangent === void 0;
    const r = t.attributes.color !== void 0;
    const s = t.attributes.normal === void 0;
    if (e.isPoints) {
      const o = "PointsMaterial:" + n.uuid;
      let a = this.cache.get(o);
      a || (a = new Rk(), tu.prototype.copy.call(a, n), a.color.copy(n.color), a.map = n.map, a.sizeAttenuation = !1, this.cache.add(o, a));
      n = a;
    } else {
      if (e.isLine) {
        const o = "LineBasicMaterial:" + n.uuid;
        let a = this.cache.get(o);
        a || (a = new uh(), tu.prototype.copy.call(a, n), a.color.copy(n.color), a.map = n.map, this.cache.add(o, a));
        n = a;
      }
    }
    if (i || r || s) {
      let o = "ClonedMaterial:" + n.uuid + ":";
      i && (o += "derivative-tangents:");
      r && (o += "vertex-colors:");
      s && (o += "flat-shading:");
      let a = this.cache.get(o);
      a || (a = n.clone(), r && (a.vertexColors = !0), s && (a.flatShading = !0), i && (a.normalScale && (a.normalScale.y *= -1), a.clearcoatNormalScale && (a.clearcoatNormalScale.y *= -1)), this.cache.add(o, a), this.associations.set(a, this.associations.get(n)));
      n = a;
    }
    e.material = n;
  }
  getMaterialType() {
    return bf;
  }
  loadMaterial(e) {
    const t = this;
    const n = this.json;
    const i = this.extensions;
    const r = n.materials[e];
    let s;
    const o = {};
    const a = r.extensions || {};
    const l = [];
    if (a[ss.KHR_MATERIALS_UNLIT]) {
      const d = i[ss.KHR_MATERIALS_UNLIT];
      s = d.getMaterialType();
      l.push(d.extendParams(o, r, t));
    } else {
      const d = r.pbrMetallicRoughness || {};
      o.color = new Bn(1, 1, 1);
      o.opacity = 1;
      if (Array.isArray(d.baseColorFactor)) {
        const h = d.baseColorFactor;
        o.color.setRGB(h[0], h[1], h[2], $v);
        o.opacity = h[3];
      }
      d.baseColorTexture !== void 0 && l.push(t.assignTexture(o, "map", d.baseColorTexture, Gb));
      o.metalness = d.metallicFactor !== void 0 ? d.metallicFactor : 1;
      o.roughness = d.roughnessFactor !== void 0 ? d.roughnessFactor : 1;
      d.metallicRoughnessTexture !== void 0 && (l.push(t.assignTexture(o, "metalnessMap", d.metallicRoughnessTexture)), l.push(t.assignTexture(o, "roughnessMap", d.metallicRoughnessTexture)));
      s = this._invokeOne(function (h) {
        return h.getMaterialType && h.getMaterialType(e);
      });
      l.push(Promise.all(this._invokeAll(function (h) {
        return h.extendMaterialParams && h.extendMaterialParams(e, o);
      })));
    }
    r.doubleSided === !0 && (o.side = 2);
    const u = r.alphaMode || m6.OPAQUE;
    u === m6.BLEND ? (o.transparent = !0, o.depthWrite = !1) : (o.transparent = !1, u === m6.MASK && (o.alphaTest = r.alphaCutoff !== void 0 ? r.alphaCutoff : .5));
    if (r.normalTexture !== void 0 && s !== ql && (l.push(t.assignTexture(o, "normalMap", r.normalTexture)), o.normalScale = new Gt(1, 1), r.normalTexture.scale !== void 0)) {
      const d = r.normalTexture.scale;
      o.normalScale.set(d, d);
    }
    r.occlusionTexture !== void 0 && s !== ql && (l.push(t.assignTexture(o, "aoMap", r.occlusionTexture)), r.occlusionTexture.strength !== void 0 && (o.aoMapIntensity = r.occlusionTexture.strength));
    if (r.emissiveFactor !== void 0 && s !== ql) {
      const d = r.emissiveFactor;
      o.emissive = new Bn().setRGB(d[0], d[1], d[2], $v);
    }
    r.emissiveTexture !== void 0 && s !== ql && l.push(t.assignTexture(o, "emissiveMap", r.emissiveTexture, Gb));
    return Promise.all(l).then(function () {
      const d = new s(o);
      r.name && (d.name = r.name);
      Pv(d, r);
      t.associations.set(d, {
        materials: e
      });
      r.extensions && fb(i, d, r);
      return d;
    });
  }
  createUniqueName(e) {
    const t = bo.sanitizeNodeName(e || "");
    return t in this.nodeNamesUsed ? t + "_" + ++this.nodeNamesUsed[t] : (this.nodeNamesUsed[t] = 0, t);
  }
  loadGeometries(e) {
    const t = this;
    const n = this.extensions;
    const i = this.primitiveCache;
    function r(o) {
      return n[ss.KHR_DRACO_MESH_COMPRESSION].decodePrimitive(o, t).then(function (a) {
        return TW(a, o, t);
      });
    }
    const s = [];
    for (let o = 0, a = e.length; o < a; o++) {
      const l = e[o];
      const u = WTe(l);
      const d = i[u];
      if (d) {
        s.push(d.promise);
      } else {
        let h;
        l.extensions && l.extensions[ss.KHR_DRACO_MESH_COMPRESSION] ? h = r(l) : h = TW(new qi(), l, t);
        i[u] = {
          primitive: l,
          promise: h
        };
        s.push(h);
      }
    }
    return Promise.all(s);
  }
  loadMesh(e) {
    const t = this;
    const n = this.json;
    const i = this.extensions;
    const r = n.meshes[e];
    const s = r.primitives;
    const o = [];
    for (let a = 0, l = s.length; a < l; a++) {
      const u = s[a].material === void 0 ? HTe(this.cache) : this.getDependency("material", s[a].material);
      o.push(u);
    }
    o.push(t.loadGeometries(s));
    return Promise.all(o).then(function (a) {
      const l = a.slice(0, a.length - 1);
      const u = a[a.length - 1];
      const d = [];
      for (let p = 0, A = u.length; p < A; p++) {
        const y = u[p];
        const E = s[p];
        let b;
        const w = l[p];
        if (E.mode === Gg.TRIANGLES || E.mode === Gg.TRIANGLE_STRIP || E.mode === Gg.TRIANGLE_FAN || E.mode === void 0) {
          b = r.isSkinnedMesh === !0 ? new gF(y, w) : new nr(y, w);
          b.isSkinnedMesh === !0 && b.normalizeSkinWeights();
          E.mode === Gg.TRIANGLE_STRIP ? b.geometry = SW(b.geometry, 1) : E.mode === Gg.TRIANGLE_FAN && (b.geometry = SW(b.geometry, 2));
        } else {
          if (E.mode === Gg.LINES) {
            b = new Im(y, w);
          } else {
            if (E.mode === Gg.LINE_STRIP) {
              b = new hy(y, w);
            } else {
              if (E.mode === Gg.LINE_LOOP) {
                b = new vF(y, w);
              } else {
                if (E.mode === Gg.POINTS) {
                  b = new yF(y, w);
                } else {
                  throw new Error("THREE.GLTFLoader: Primitive mode unsupported: " + E.mode);
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
        Object.keys(b.geometry.morphAttributes).length > 0 && GTe(b, r);
        b.name = t.createUniqueName(r.name || "mesh_" + e);
        Pv(b, r);
        E.extensions && fb(i, b, E);
        t.assignFinalMaterial(b);
        d.push(b);
      }
      for (let p = 0, A = d.length; p < A; p++) {
        t.associations.set(d[p], {
          meshes: e,
          primitives: p
        });
      }
      if (d.length === 1) {
        r.extensions && fb(i, d[0], r);
        return d[0];
      }
      const h = new Vu();
      r.extensions && fb(i, h, r);
      t.associations.set(h, {
        meshes: e
      });
      for (let p = 0, A = d.length; p < A; p++) {
        h.add(d[p]);
      }
      return h;
    });
  }
  loadCamera(e) {
    let t;
    const n = this.json.cameras[e];
    const i = n[n.type];
    if (!i) {
      console.warn("THREE.GLTFLoader: Missing camera parameters.");
      return;
    }
    n.type === "perspective" ? t = new Au(Eee.radToDeg(i.yfov), i.aspectRatio || 1, i.znear || 1, i.zfar || 2e6) : n.type === "orthographic" && (t = new OC(-i.xmag, i.xmag, i.ymag, -i.ymag, i.znear, i.zfar));
    n.name && (t.name = this.createUniqueName(n.name));
    Pv(t, n);
    return Promise.resolve(t);
  }
  loadSkin(e) {
    const t = this.json.skins[e];
    const n = [];
    for (let i = 0, r = t.joints.length; i < r; i++) {
      n.push(this._loadNodeShallow(t.joints[i]));
    }
    t.inverseBindMatrices !== void 0 ? n.push(this.getDependency("accessor", t.inverseBindMatrices)) : n.push(null);
    return Promise.all(n).then(function (i) {
      const r = i.pop();
      const s = i;
      const o = [];
      const a = [];
      for (let l = 0, u = s.length; l < u; l++) {
        const d = s[l];
        if (d) {
          o.push(d);
          const h = new Fn();
          r !== null && h.fromArray(r.array, l * 16);
          a.push(h);
        } else {
          console.warn('THREE.GLTFLoader: Joint "%s" could not be found.', t.joints[l]);
        }
      }
      return new AF(o, a);
    });
  }
  loadAnimation(e) {
    const t = this.json;
    const n = this;
    const i = t.animations[e];
    const r = i.name ? i.name : "animation_" + e;
    const s = [];
    const o = [];
    const a = [];
    const l = [];
    const u = [];
    for (let d = 0, h = i.channels.length; d < h; d++) {
      const p = i.channels[d];
      const A = i.samplers[p.sampler];
      const y = p.target;
      const E = y.node;
      const b = i.parameters !== void 0 ? i.parameters[A.input] : A.input;
      const w = i.parameters !== void 0 ? i.parameters[A.output] : A.output;
      y.node !== void 0 && (s.push(this.getDependency("node", E)), o.push(this.getDependency("accessor", b)), a.push(this.getDependency("accessor", w)), l.push(A), u.push(y));
    }
    return Promise.all([Promise.all(s), Promise.all(o), Promise.all(a), Promise.all(l), Promise.all(u)]).then(function (d) {
      const h = d[0];
      const p = d[1];
      const A = d[2];
      const y = d[3];
      const E = d[4];
      const b = [];
      for (let w = 0, T = h.length; w < T; w++) {
        const x = h[w];
        const R = p[w];
        const D = A[w];
        const O = y[w];
        const z = E[w];
        if (x === void 0) {
          continue;
        }
        x.updateMatrix && x.updateMatrix();
        const Q = n._createAnimationTracks(x, R, D, O, z);
        if (Q) {
          for (let G = 0; G < Q.length; G++) {
            b.push(Q[G]);
          }
        }
      }
      return new hC(r, void 0, b);
    });
  }
  createNodeMesh(e) {
    const t = this.json;
    const n = this;
    const i = t.nodes[e];
    return i.mesh === void 0 ? null : n.getDependency("mesh", i.mesh).then(function (r) {
      const s = n._getNodeRef(n.meshCache, i.mesh, r);
      i.weights !== void 0 && s.traverse(function (o) {
        if (o.isMesh) {
          for (let a = 0, l = i.weights.length; a < l; a++) {
            o.morphTargetInfluences[a] = i.weights[a];
          }
        }
      });
      return s;
    });
  }
  loadNode(e) {
    const t = this.json;
    const n = this;
    const i = t.nodes[e];
    const r = n._loadNodeShallow(e);
    const s = [];
    const o = i.children || [];
    for (let l = 0, u = o.length; l < u; l++) {
      s.push(n.getDependency("node", o[l]));
    }
    const a = i.skin === void 0 ? Promise.resolve(null) : n.getDependency("skin", i.skin);
    return Promise.all([r, Promise.all(s), a]).then(function (l) {
      const u = l[0];
      const d = l[1];
      const h = l[2];
      h !== null && u.traverse(function (p) {
        p.isSkinnedMesh && p.bind(h, jTe);
      });
      for (let p = 0, A = d.length; p < A; p++) {
        u.add(d[p]);
      }
      return u;
    });
  }
  _loadNodeShallow(e) {
    const t = this.json;
    const n = this.extensions;
    const i = this;
    if (this.nodeCache[e] !== void 0) {
      return this.nodeCache[e];
    }
    const r = t.nodes[e];
    const s = r.name ? i.createUniqueName(r.name) : "";
    const o = [];
    const a = i._invokeOne(function (l) {
      return l.createNodeMesh && l.createNodeMesh(e);
    });
    a && o.push(a);
    r.camera !== void 0 && o.push(i.getDependency("camera", r.camera).then(function (l) {
      return i._getNodeRef(i.cameraCache, r.camera, l);
    }));
    i._invokeAll(function (l) {
      return l.createNodeAttachment && l.createNodeAttachment(e);
    }).forEach(function (l) {
      o.push(l);
    });
    this.nodeCache[e] = Promise.all(o).then(function (l) {
      let u;
      r.isBone === !0 ? u = new Mk() : l.length > 1 ? u = new Vu() : l.length === 1 ? u = l[0] : u = new Os();
      if (u !== l[0]) {
        for (let d = 0, h = l.length; d < h; d++) {
          u.add(l[d]);
        }
      }
      r.name && (u.userData.name = r.name, u.name = s);
      Pv(u, r);
      r.extensions && fb(n, u, r);
      if (r.matrix !== void 0) {
        const d = new Fn();
        d.fromArray(r.matrix);
        u.applyMatrix4(d);
      } else {
        r.translation !== void 0 && u.position.fromArray(r.translation);
        r.rotation !== void 0 && u.quaternion.fromArray(r.rotation);
        r.scale !== void 0 && u.scale.fromArray(r.scale);
      }
      i.associations.has(u) || i.associations.set(u, {});
      i.associations.get(u).nodes = e;
      return u;
    });
    return this.nodeCache[e];
  }
  loadScene(e) {
    const t = this.extensions;
    const n = this.json.scenes[e];
    const i = this;
    const r = new Vu();
    n.name && (r.name = i.createUniqueName(n.name));
    Pv(r, n);
    n.extensions && fb(t, r, n);
    const s = n.nodes || [];
    const o = [];
    for (let a = 0, l = s.length; a < l; a++) {
      o.push(i.getDependency("node", s[a]));
    }
    return Promise.all(o).then(function (a) {
      for (let u = 0, d = a.length; u < d; u++) {
        r.add(a[u]);
      }
      const l = u => {
        const d = new Map();
        for (const [h, p] of i.associations) {
          (h instanceof tu || h instanceof wl) && d.set(h, p);
        }
        u.traverse(h => {
          const p = i.associations.get(h);
          p != null && d.set(h, p);
        });
        return d;
      };
      i.associations = l(r);
      return r;
    });
  }
  _createAnimationTracks(e, t, n, i, r) {
    const s = [];
    const o = e.name ? e.name : e.uuid;
    const a = [];
    A1[r.path] === A1.weights ? e.traverse(function (h) {
      h.morphTargetInfluences && a.push(h.name ? h.name : h.uuid);
    }) : a.push(o);
    let l;
    switch (A1[r.path]) {
      case A1.weights:
        l = cC;
        break;
      case A1.rotation:
        l = uC;
        break;
      case A1.position:
      case A1.scale:
        l = dC;
        break;
      default:
        n.itemSize === 1 ? l = cC : l = dC;
    }
    const u = i.interpolation !== void 0 ? QTe[i.interpolation] : Aw;
    const d = this._getArrayFromAccessor(n);
    for (let h = 0, p = a.length; h < p; h++) {
      const A = new l(a[h] + "." + A1[r.path], t.array, d, u);
      i.interpolation === "CUBICSPLINE" && this._createCubicSplineTrackInterpolant(A);
      s.push(A);
    }
    return s;
  }
  _getArrayFromAccessor(e) {
    let t = e.array;
    if (e.normalized) {
      const n = kN(t.constructor);
      const i = new Float32Array(t.length);
      for (let r = 0, s = t.length; r < s; r++) {
        i[r] = t[r] * n;
      }
      t = i;
    }
    return t;
  }
  _createCubicSplineTrackInterpolant(e) {
    e.createInterpolant = function (n) {
      return new (this instanceof uC ? FTe : Une)(this.times, this.values, this.getValueSize() / 3, n);
    };
    e.createInterpolant.isInterpolantFactoryMethodGLTFCubicSpline = !0;
  }
};
function YTe(e, t, n) {
  const i = t.attributes;
  const r = new ur();
  if (i.POSITION !== void 0) {
    const a = n.json.accessors[i.POSITION];
    const l = a.min;
    const u = a.max;
    if (l !== void 0 && u !== void 0) {
      r.set(new ve(l[0], l[1], l[2]), new ve(u[0], u[1], u[2]));
      if (a.normalized) {
        const d = kN(LS[a.componentType]);
        r.min.multiplyScalar(d);
        r.max.multiplyScalar(d);
      }
    } else {
      console.warn("THREE.GLTFLoader: Missing min/max properties for accessor POSITION.");
      return;
    }
  } else {
    return;
  }
  const s = t.targets;
  if (s !== void 0) {
    const a = new ve();
    const l = new ve();
    for (let u = 0, d = s.length; u < d; u++) {
      const h = s[u];
      if (h.POSITION !== void 0) {
        const p = n.json.accessors[h.POSITION];
        const A = p.min;
        const y = p.max;
        if (A !== void 0 && y !== void 0) {
          l.setX(Math.max(Math.abs(A[0]), Math.abs(y[0])));
          l.setY(Math.max(Math.abs(A[1]), Math.abs(y[1])));
          l.setZ(Math.max(Math.abs(A[2]), Math.abs(y[2])));
          if (p.normalized) {
            const E = kN(LS[p.componentType]);
            l.multiplyScalar(E);
          }
          a.max(l);
        } else {
          console.warn("THREE.GLTFLoader: Missing min/max properties for accessor POSITION.");
        }
      }
    }
    r.expandByVector(a);
  }
  e.boundingBox = r;
  const o = new Fa();
  r.getCenter(o.center);
  o.radius = r.min.distanceTo(r.max) / 2;
  e.boundingSphere = o;
}
function TW(e, t, n) {
  const i = t.attributes;
  const r = [];
  function s(o, a) {
    return n.getDependency("accessor", o).then(function (l) {
      e.setAttribute(a, l);
    });
  }
  for (const o in i) {
    const a = RN[o] || o.toLowerCase();
    a in e.attributes || r.push(s(i[o], a));
  }
  if (t.indices !== void 0 && !e.index) {
    const o = n.getDependency("accessor", t.indices).then(function (a) {
      e.setIndex(a);
    });
    r.push(o);
  }
  Pv(e, t);
  YTe(e, t, n);
  return Promise.all(r).then(function () {
    return t.targets !== void 0 ? zTe(e, t.targets, n) : e;
  });
}

var JTe = class extends Id {
  constructor(e) {

    this.workerPool = [];

      position: "POSITION",
      normal: "NORMAL",
      color: "COLOR",
      uv: "TEX_COORD"
    };
    this.defaultAttributeTypes = {
      position: "Float32Array",
      normal: "Float32Array",
      color: "Float32Array",
      uv: "Float32Array"
    };
  }
  setDecoderPath(e) {
    this.decoderPath = e;
    return this;
  }
  setDecoderConfig(e) {
    this.decoderConfig = e;
    return this;
  }
  setWorkerLimit(e) {
    this.workerLimit = e;
    return this;
  }
  load(e, t, n, i) {
    const r = new Bf(this.manager);
    r.setPath(this.path);
    r.setResponseType("arraybuffer");
    r.setRequestHeader(this.requestHeader);
    r.setWithCredentials(this.withCredentials);
    r.load(e, s => {
      const o = {
        attributeIDs: this.defaultAttributeIDs,
        attributeTypes: this.defaultAttributeTypes,
        useUniqueIDs: !1
      };
      this.decodeGeometry(s, o).then(t).catch(i);
    }, n, i);
  }
  decodeDracoFile(e, t, n, i) {
    const r = {
      attributeIDs: n || this.defaultAttributeIDs,
      attributeTypes: i || this.defaultAttributeTypes,
      useUniqueIDs: !!n
    };
    this.decodeGeometry(e, r).then(t);
  }
  decodeGeometry(e, t) {
    for (const a in t.attributeTypes) {
      const l = t.attributeTypes[a];
      l.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT !== void 0 && (t.attributeTypes[a] = l.name);
    }
    const n = JSON.stringify(t);
    if (y6.has(e)) {
      const a = y6.get(e);
      if (a.key === n) {
        return a.promise;
      }
      if (e.byteLength === 0) {
        throw new Error("THREE.DRACOLoader: Unable to re-decode a buffer with different settings. Buffer has already been transferred.");
      }
    }
    let i;
    const r = this.workerNextTaskID++;
    const s = e.byteLength;
    const o = this._getWorker(r, s).then(a => {
      i = a;
      return new Promise((l, u) => {
        i._callbacks[r] = {
          resolve: l,
          reject: u
        };
        i.postMessage({
          type: "decode",
          id: r,
          taskConfig: t,
          buffer: e
        }, [e]);
      });
    }).then(a => this._createGeometry(a.geometry));
    o.catch(() => !0).then(() => {
      i && r && this._releaseTask(i, r);
    });
    y6.set(e, {
      key: n,
      promise: o
    });
    return o;
  }
  _createGeometry(e) {
    const t = new qi();
    e.index && t.setIndex(new Jr(e.index.array, 1));
    for (let n = 0; n < e.attributes.length; n++) {
      const i = e.attributes[n];
      const r = i.name;
      const s = i.array;
      const o = i.itemSize;
      t.setAttribute(r, new Jr(s, o));
    }
    return t;
  }
  _loadLibrary(e, t) {
    const n = new Bf(this.manager);
    n.setPath(this.decoderPath);
    n.setResponseType(t);
    n.setWithCredentials(this.withCredentials);
    return new Promise((i, r) => {
      n.load(e, i, void 0, r);
    });
  }
  preload() {
    this._initDecoder();
    return this;
  }
  _initDecoder() {
    if (this.decoderPending) {
      return this.decoderPending;
    }
    const e = typeof WebAssembly != "object" || this.decoderConfig.type === "js";
    const t = [];
    e ? t.push(this._loadLibrary("draco_decoder.js", "text")) : (t.push(this._loadLibrary("draco_wasm_wrapper.js", "text")), t.push(this._loadLibrary("draco_decoder.wasm", "arraybuffer")));
    this.decoderPending = Promise.all(t).then(n => {
      const i = n[0];
      e || (this.decoderConfig.wasmBinary = n[1]);
      const r = XTe.toString();
      const s = ["/* draco decoder */", i, "", "/* worker */", r.substring(r.indexOf("{") + 1, r.lastIndexOf("}"))].join(`
`);
      this.workerSourceURL = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([s]));
    });
    return this.decoderPending;
  }
  _getWorker(e, t) {
    return this._initDecoder().then(() => {
      if (this.workerPool.length < this.workerLimit) {
        const i = new Worker(this.workerSourceURL);
        i._callbacks = {};
        i._taskCosts = {};
        i._taskLoad = 0;
        i.postMessage({
          type: "init",
          decoderConfig: this.decoderConfig
        });
        i.onmessage = function (r) {
          const s = r.data;
          switch (s.type) {
            case "decode":
              i._callbacks[s.id].resolve(s);
              break;
            case "error":
              i._callbacks[s.id].reject(s);
              break;
            default:
              console.error('THREE.DRACOLoader: Unexpected message, "' + s.type + '"');
          }
        };
        this.workerPool.push(i);
      } else {
        this.workerPool.sort(function (i, r) {
          return i._taskLoad > r._taskLoad ? -1 : 1;
        });
      }
      const n = this.workerPool[this.workerPool.length - 1];
      n._taskCosts[e] = t;
      n._taskLoad += t;
      return n;
    });
  }
  _releaseTask(e, t) {
    e._taskLoad -= e._taskCosts[t];
    delete e._callbacks[t];
    delete e._taskCosts[t];
  }
  debug() {
    console.log("Task load: ", this.workerPool.map(e => e._taskLoad));
  }
  dispose() {
    for (let e = 0; e < this.workerPool.length; ++e) {
      this.workerPool[e].terminate();
    }
    this.workerPool.length = 0;
    return this;
  }
};
function XTe() {
  let e;
  let t;
  onmessage = function (o) {
    const a = o.data;
    switch (a.type) {
      case "init":

        t = new Promise(function (d) {
          e.onModuleLoaded = function (h) {
            d({
              draco: h
            });
          };

        });

          const h = d.draco;

            y.index && E.push(y.index.array.buffer);
            self.postMessage({
              type: "decode",
              id: a.id,
              geometry: y
            }, E);
          } catch (y) {
            console.error(y);
            self.postMessage({
              type: "error",
              id: a.id,
              error: y.message
            });
          } finally {
            h.destroy(A);
            h.destroy(p);
          }
        });
    }
  };
  function n(o, a, l, u) {
    const d = u.attributeIDs;
    const h = u.attributeTypes;
    let p;
    let A;
    const y = a.GetEncodedGeometryType(l);
    if (y === o.TRIANGULAR_MESH) {
      p = new o.Mesh();
      A = a.DecodeBufferToMesh(l, p);
    } else {
      if (y === o.POINT_CLOUD) {
        p = new o.PointCloud();
        A = a.DecodeBufferToPointCloud(l, p);
      } else {
        throw new Error("THREE.DRACOLoader: Unexpected geometry type.");
      }
    }
    if (!A.ok() || p.ptr === 0) {
      throw new Error("THREE.DRACOLoader: Decoding failed: " + A.error_msg());
    }
    const E = {
      index: null,
      attributes: []
    };
    for (const b in d) {
      const w = self[h[b]];
      let T;
      let x;
      if (u.useUniqueIDs) {
        x = d[b];
        T = a.GetAttributeByUniqueId(p, x);
      } else {
        x = a.GetAttributeId(p, o[d[b]]);
        if (x === -1) {
          continue;
        }
        T = a.GetAttribute(p, x);
      }
      E.attributes.push(r(o, a, p, b, w, T));
    }
    y === o.TRIANGULAR_MESH && (E.index = i(o, a, p));
    o.destroy(p);
    return E;
  }
  function i(o, a, l) {
    const u = l.num_faces() * 3;
    const d = u * 4;
    const h = o._malloc(d);
    a.GetTrianglesUInt32Array(l, d, h);
    const p = new Uint32Array(o.HEAPF32.buffer, h, u).slice();
    o._free(h);
    return {
      array: p,
      itemSize: 1
    };
  }
  function r(o, a, l, u, d, h) {
    const p = h.num_components();
    const A = l.num_points() * p;
    const y = A * d.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT;
    const E = s(o, d);
    const b = o._malloc(y);
    a.GetAttributeDataArrayForAllPoints(l, h, E, y, b);
    const w = new d(o.HEAPF32.buffer, b, A).slice();
    o._free(b);
    return {
      name: u,
      array: w,
      itemSize: p
    };
  }
  function s(o, a) {
    switch (a) {
      case Float32Array:
        return o.DT_FLOAT32;
      case Int8Array:
        return o.DT_INT8;
      case Int16Array:
        return o.DT_INT16;
      case Int32Array:
        return o.DT_INT32;
      case Uint8Array:
        return o.DT_UINT8;
      case Uint16Array:
        return o.DT_UINT16;
      case Uint32Array:
        return o.DT_UINT32;
    }
  }

function OW(e) {

      return null;

function LN(e) {

function b3(e, t, n) {

function M6(e, t, n, i, r) {

  const l = s.pos;
  const u = s.axis * 2;
  const d = n.offset || 0;
  for (;;) {
    for (; o <= a && n[(o - d) * 6 + u] < l;) {
      o++;
    }
    for (; o <= a && n[(a - d) * 6 + u] >= l;) {
      a--;
    }
    if (o < a) {
      for (let h = 0; h < t; h++) {
        let p = e[o * t + h];
        e[o * t + h] = e[a * t + h];
        e[a * t + h] = p;
      }
      for (let h = 0; h < 6; h++) {
        const p = o - d;
        const A = a - d;
        const y = n[p * 6 + h];
        n[p * 6 + h] = n[A * 6 + h];
        n[A * 6 + h] = y;
      }
      o++;
      a--;
    } else {
      return o;
    }
  }
}

function UN(e, t) {

      right: a,

    } = t;

  }

function XBe(e, t, n, i, r, s) {

    const b = A + (isFinite(y) ? y : l - A);
    E < a && b > o && (u.push({
      pos: Math.max(o, E),
      isStart: !0
    }), u.push({
      pos: Math.min(a, b),
      isStart: !1
    }));
  }
  u.sort((p, A) => p.pos !== A.pos ? p.pos - A.pos : p.type === "end" ? -1 : 1);
  let d = 0;
  let h = null;
  for (const p of u) {
    const A = p.pos;
    d !== 0 && A !== h && s.push({
      offset: h,
      count: A - h
    });
    d += p.isStart ? 1 : -1;
    h = A;
  }
  return s;
}
function a3e(e, t) {
  const n = e[e.length - 1];
  const i = n.offset + n.count > 2 ** 16;
  const r = e.reduce((u, d) => u + d.count, 0);
  const s = i ? 4 : 2;
  const o = t ? new SharedArrayBuffer(r * s) : new ArrayBuffer(r * s);
  const a = i ? new Uint32Array(o) : new Uint16Array(o);
  let l = 0;
  for (let u = 0; u < e.length; u++) {
    const {
      offset: d,
      count: h
    } = e[u];
    for (let p = 0; p < h; p++) {
      a[l + p] = d + p;
    }
    l += h;
  }
  return a;
}
var l3e = class extends t3e {
  get indirect() {
    return !!this._indirectBuffer;
  }
  get primitiveStride() {
    return null;
  }
  get primitiveBufferStride() {
    return this.indirect ? 1 : this.primitiveStride;
  }
  set primitiveBufferStride(e) {}
  get primitiveBuffer() {
    return this.indirect ? this._indirectBuffer : this.geometry.index.array;
  }
  set primitiveBuffer(e) {}
  constructor(e, t = {}) {
    if (e.isBufferGeometry) {
      if (e.index && e.index.isInterleavedBufferAttribute) {
        throw new Error("BVH: InterleavedBufferAttribute is not supported for the index attribute.");
      }
    } else {
      throw new Error("BVH: Only BufferGeometries are supported.");
    }
    if (t.useSharedArrayBuffer && !n3e()) {
      throw new Error("BVH: SharedArrayBuffer is not available.");

    } = this;

      this._indirectBuffer = i;
    } else {
      r3e(t, e);
    }
    super.init(e);
    !t.boundingBox && e.setBoundingBox && (t.boundingBox = this.getBoundingBox(new ur()));
  }
  getRootRanges(e) {
    return this.indirect ? [{
      offset: 0,

    }] : HW(this.geometry, e, this.primitiveStride);
  }
  raycastObject3D() {
    throw new Error("BVH: raycastObject3D() not implemented");
  }

var fy = class {
  constructor() {
    this.min = 1 / 0;
    this.max = -1 / 0;
  }
  setFromPointsField(e, t) {
    let n = 1 / 0;
    let i = -1 / 0;
    for (let r = 0, s = e.length; r < s; r++) {
      const o = e[r][t];
      n = o < n ? o : n;
      i = o > i ? o : i;
    }
    this.min = n;
    this.max = i;
  }
  setFromPoints(e, t) {
    let n = 1 / 0;
    let i = -1 / 0;
    for (let r = 0, s = t.length; r < s; r++) {
      const o = t[r];
      const a = e.dot(o);
      n = a < n ? a : n;
      i = a > i ? a : i;
    }
    this.min = n;
    this.max = i;
  }
  isSeparated(e) {
    return this.min > e.max || e.min > this.max;
  }
};
fy.prototype.setFromBox = function () {
  const e = new ve();
  return function (n, i) {
    const r = i.min;
    const s = i.max;
    let o = 1 / 0;
    let a = -1 / 0;
    for (let l = 0; l <= 1; l++) {
      for (let u = 0; u <= 1; u++) {
        for (let d = 0; d <= 1; d++) {
          e.x = r.x * l + s.x * (1 - l);
          e.y = r.y * u + s.y * (1 - u);
          e.z = r.z * d + s.z * (1 - d);
          const h = n.dot(e);
          o = Math.min(h, o);
          a = Math.max(h, a);
        }
      }
    }
    this.min = o;
    this.max = a;
  };
}();
var c3e = function () {
  const e = new ve();
  const t = new ve();
  const n = new ve();
  return function (r, s, o) {
    const a = r.start;
    const l = e;
    const u = s.start;
    const d = t;
    n.subVectors(a, u);
    e.subVectors(r.end, r.start);
    t.subVectors(s.end, s.start);
    const h = n.dot(d);
    const p = d.dot(l);
    const A = d.dot(d);
    const y = n.dot(l);
    const E = l.dot(l) * A - p * p;
    let b;
    let w;
    E !== 0 ? b = (h * p - y * A) / E : b = 0;
    w = (h + b * p) / A;
    o.x = b;
    o.y = w;
  };
}();
var g9 = function () {
  const e = new Gt();
  const t = new ve();
  const n = new ve();
  return function (r, s, o, a) {
    c3e(r, s, e);
    let l = e.x;
    let u = e.y;
    if (l >= 0 && l <= 1 && u >= 0 && u <= 1) {
      r.at(l, o);
      s.at(u, a);
      return;
    } else {
      if (l >= 0 && l <= 1) {
        u < 0 ? s.at(0, a) : s.at(1, a);
        r.closestPointToPoint(a, !0, o);
        return;
      } else {
        if (u >= 0 && u <= 1) {
          l < 0 ? r.at(0, o) : r.at(1, o);
          s.closestPointToPoint(o, !0, a);
          return;
        } else {
          let d;
          l < 0 ? d = r.start : d = r.end;
          let h;
          u < 0 ? h = s.start : h = s.end;
          const p = t;
          const A = n;
          r.closestPointToPoint(h, !0, t);
          s.closestPointToPoint(d, !0, n);
          if (p.distanceToSquared(h) <= A.distanceToSquared(d)) {
            o.copy(p);
            a.copy(h);
            return;
          } else {
            o.copy(d);
            a.copy(A);
            return;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  };
}();
var u3e = function () {
  const e = new ve();
  const t = new ve();
  const n = new bl();
  const i = new fm();
  return function (s, o) {
    const {
      radius: a,
      center: l
    } = s;
    const {
      a: u,
      b: d,
      c: h
    } = o;
    i.start = u;
    i.end = d;
    if (i.closestPointToPoint(l, !0, e).distanceTo(l) <= a || (i.start = u, i.end = h, i.closestPointToPoint(l, !0, e).distanceTo(l) <= a) || (i.start = d, i.end = h, i.closestPointToPoint(l, !0, e).distanceTo(l) <= a)) {
      return !0;
    }
    const p = o.getPlane(n);
    if (Math.abs(p.distanceToPoint(l)) <= a) {
      const A = p.projectPoint(l, t);
      if (o.containsPoint(A)) {
        return !0;
      }
    }
    return !1;
  };
}();
var d3e = ["x", "y", "z"];
var Uv = 1e-15;
var zW = Uv * Uv;
function Fg(e) {
  return Math.abs(e) < Uv;
}
var pm = class extends wf {
  constructor(...e) {
    super(...e);
    this.isExtendedTriangle = !0;
    this.satAxes = new Array(4).fill().map(() => new ve());
    this.satBounds = new Array(4).fill().map(() => new fy());
    this.points = [this.a, this.b, this.c];
    this.plane = new bl();
    this.isDegenerateIntoSegment = !1;
    this.isDegenerateIntoPoint = !1;
    this.degenerateSegment = new fm();
    this.needsUpdate = !0;
  }
  intersectsSphere(e) {
    return u3e(e, this);
  }
  update() {
    const e = this.a;
    const t = this.b;
    const n = this.c;
    const i = this.points;
    const r = this.satAxes;
    const s = this.satBounds;
    const o = r[0];
    const a = s[0];
    this.getNormal(o);
    a.setFromPoints(o, i);
    const l = r[1];
    const u = s[1];
    l.subVectors(e, t);
    u.setFromPoints(l, i);
    const d = r[2];
    const h = s[2];
    d.subVectors(t, n);
    h.setFromPoints(d, i);
    const p = r[3];
    const A = s[3];
    p.subVectors(n, e);
    A.setFromPoints(p, i);
    const y = l.length();
    const E = d.length();
    const b = p.length();
    this.isDegenerateIntoPoint = !1;
    this.isDegenerateIntoSegment = !1;
    y < Uv ? E < Uv || b < Uv ? this.isDegenerateIntoPoint = !0 : (this.isDegenerateIntoSegment = !0, this.degenerateSegment.start.copy(e), this.degenerateSegment.end.copy(n)) : E < Uv ? b < Uv ? this.isDegenerateIntoPoint = !0 : (this.isDegenerateIntoSegment = !0, this.degenerateSegment.start.copy(t), this.degenerateSegment.end.copy(e)) : b < Uv && (this.isDegenerateIntoSegment = !0, this.degenerateSegment.start.copy(n), this.degenerateSegment.end.copy(t));
    this.plane.setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(o, e);
    this.needsUpdate = !1;
  }
};
pm.prototype.closestPointToSegment = function () {
  const e = new ve();
  const t = new ve();
  const n = new fm();
  return function (r, s = null, o = null) {
    const {
      start: a,
      end: l
    } = r;
    const u = this.points;
    let d;
    let h = 1 / 0;
    for (let p = 0; p < 3; p++) {
      const A = (p + 1) % 3;
      n.start.copy(u[p]);
      n.end.copy(u[A]);
      g9(n, r, e, t);
      d = e.distanceToSquared(t);
      d < h && (h = d, s && s.copy(e), o && o.copy(t));
    }
    this.closestPointToPoint(a, e);
    d = a.distanceToSquared(e);
    d < h && (h = d, s && s.copy(e), o && o.copy(a));
    this.closestPointToPoint(l, e);
    d = l.distanceToSquared(e);
    d < h && (h = d, s && s.copy(e), o && o.copy(l));
    return Math.sqrt(h);
  };
}();
pm.prototype.intersectsTriangle = function () {
  const e = new pm();
  const t = new fy();
  const n = new fy();
  const i = new ve();
  const r = new ve();
  const s = new ve();
  const o = new ve();
  const a = new fm();
  const l = new fm();
  const u = new ve();
  const d = new Gt();
  const h = new Gt();
  function p(x, R, D, O) {
    const z = i;
    !x.isDegenerateIntoPoint && !x.isDegenerateIntoSegment ? z.copy(x.plane.normal) : z.copy(R.plane.normal);
    const Q = x.satBounds;
    const G = x.satAxes;
    for (let H = 1; H < 4; H++) {
      const ee = Q[H];
      const $ = G[H];
      t.setFromPoints($, R.points);
      if (ee.isSeparated(t) || (o.copy(z).cross($), t.setFromPoints(o, x.points), n.setFromPoints(o, R.points), t.isSeparated(n))) {
        return !1;
      }
    }
    const N = R.satBounds;
    const F = R.satAxes;
    for (let H = 1; H < 4; H++) {
      const ee = N[H];
      const $ = F[H];
      t.setFromPoints($, x.points);
      if (ee.isSeparated(t) || (o.crossVectors(z, $), t.setFromPoints(o, x.points), n.setFromPoints(o, R.points), t.isSeparated(n))) {
        return !1;
      }
    }
    D && (O || console.warn("ExtendedTriangle.intersectsTriangle: Triangles are coplanar which does not support an output edge. Setting edge to 0, 0, 0."), D.start.set(0, 0, 0), D.end.set(0, 0, 0));
    return !0;
  }
  function A(x, R, D, O, z, Q, G, N, F, H, ee) {
    let $ = G / (G - N);
    H.x = O + (z - O) * $;
    ee.start.subVectors(R, x).multiplyScalar($).add(x);
    $ = G / (G - F);
    H.y = O + (Q - O) * $;
    ee.end.subVectors(D, x).multiplyScalar($).add(x);
  }
  function y(x, R, D, O, z, Q, G, N, F, H, ee) {
    if (z > 0) {
      A(x.c, x.a, x.b, O, R, D, F, G, N, H, ee);
    } else {
      if (Q > 0) {
        A(x.b, x.a, x.c, D, R, O, N, G, F, H, ee);
      } else {
        if (N * F > 0 || G != 0) {
          A(x.a, x.b, x.c, R, D, O, G, N, F, H, ee);
        } else {
          if (N != 0) {
            A(x.b, x.a, x.c, D, R, O, N, G, F, H, ee);
          } else {
            if (F != 0) {
              A(x.c, x.a, x.b, O, R, D, F, G, N, H, ee);
            } else {
              return !0;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return !1;
  }
  function E(x, R, D, O) {
    const z = R.degenerateSegment;
    const Q = x.plane.distanceToPoint(z.start);
    const G = x.plane.distanceToPoint(z.end);
    return Fg(Q) ? Fg(G) ? p(x, R, D, O) : (D && (D.start.copy(z.start), D.end.copy(z.start)), x.containsPoint(z.start)) : Fg(G) ? (D && (D.start.copy(z.end), D.end.copy(z.end)), x.containsPoint(z.end)) : x.plane.intersectLine(z, i) != null ? (D && (D.start.copy(i), D.end.copy(i)), x.containsPoint(i)) : !1;
  }
  function b(x, R, D) {
    const O = R.a;
    return Fg(x.plane.distanceToPoint(O)) && x.containsPoint(O) ? (D && (D.start.copy(O), D.end.copy(O)), !0) : !1;
  }
  function w(x, R, D) {
    const O = x.degenerateSegment;
    const z = R.a;
    O.closestPointToPoint(z, !0, i);
    return z.distanceToSquared(i) < zW ? (D && (D.start.copy(z), D.end.copy(z)), !0) : !1;
  }
  function T(x, R, D, O) {
    if (x.isDegenerateIntoSegment) {
      if (R.isDegenerateIntoSegment) {
        const z = x.degenerateSegment;
        const Q = R.degenerateSegment;
        const G = r;
        const N = s;
        z.delta(G);
        Q.delta(N);
        const F = i.subVectors(Q.start, z.start);
        const H = G.x * N.y - G.y * N.x;
        if (Fg(H)) {
          return !1;
        }
        const ee = (F.x * N.y - F.y * N.x) / H;
        const $ = -(G.x * F.y - G.y * F.x) / H;
        return ee < 0 || ee > 1 || $ < 0 || $ > 1 ? !1 : Fg(z.start.z + G.z * ee - (Q.start.z + N.z * $)) ? (D && (D.start.copy(z.start).addScaledVector(G, ee), D.end.copy(z.start).addScaledVector(G, ee)), !0) : !1;
      } else {
        return R.isDegenerateIntoPoint ? w(x, R, D) : E(R, x, D, O);
      }
    } else {
      if (x.isDegenerateIntoPoint) {
        return R.isDegenerateIntoPoint ? R.a.distanceToSquared(x.a) < zW ? (D && (D.start.copy(x.a), D.end.copy(x.a)), !0) : !1 : R.isDegenerateIntoSegment ? w(R, x, D) : b(R, x, D);
      }
      if (R.isDegenerateIntoPoint) {
        return b(x, R, D);
      }
      if (R.isDegenerateIntoSegment) {
        return E(x, R, D, O);
      }
    }
  }
  return function (R, D = null, O = !1) {
    this.needsUpdate && this.update();
    R.isExtendedTriangle ? R.needsUpdate && R.update() : (e.copy(R), e.update(), R = e);
    const z = T(this, R, D, O);
    if (z !== void 0) {
      return z;
    }
    const Q = this.plane;
    const G = R.plane;
    let N = G.distanceToPoint(this.a);
    let F = G.distanceToPoint(this.b);
    let H = G.distanceToPoint(this.c);
    Fg(N) && (N = 0);
    Fg(F) && (F = 0);
    Fg(H) && (H = 0);
    const ee = N * F;
    const $ = N * H;
    if (ee > 0 && $ > 0) {
      return !1;
    }
    let te = Q.distanceToPoint(R.a);
    let re = Q.distanceToPoint(R.b);
    let ne = Q.distanceToPoint(R.c);
    Fg(te) && (te = 0);
    Fg(re) && (re = 0);
    Fg(ne) && (ne = 0);
    const le = te * re;
    const me = te * ne;
    if (le > 0 && me > 0) {
      return !1;
    }
    r.copy(Q.normal);
    s.copy(G.normal);
    const Me = r.cross(s);
    let Je = 0;
    let ke = Math.abs(Me.x);
    const Z = Math.abs(Me.y);
    Z > ke && (ke = Z, Je = 1);
    Math.abs(Me.z) > ke && (Je = 2);
    const se = d3e[Je];
    const X = this.a[se];
    const pe = this.b[se];
    const ge = this.c[se];
    const Se = R.a[se];
    const Oe = R.b[se];
    const Ke = R.c[se];
    if (y(this, X, pe, ge, ee, $, N, F, H, d, a)) {
      return p(this, R, D, O);
    }
    if (y(R, Se, Oe, Ke, le, me, te, re, ne, h, l)) {
      return p(this, R, D, O);
    }
    if (d.y < d.x) {
      const at = d.y;
      d.y = d.x;
      d.x = at;
      u.copy(a.start);
      a.start.copy(a.end);
      a.end.copy(u);
    }
    if (h.y < h.x) {
      const at = h.y;
      h.y = h.x;
      h.x = at;
      u.copy(l.start);
      l.start.copy(l.end);
      l.end.copy(u);
    }
    return d.y < h.x || h.y < d.x ? !1 : (D && (h.x > d.x ? D.start.copy(l.start) : D.start.copy(a.start), h.y < d.y ? D.end.copy(l.end) : D.end.copy(a.end)), !0);
  };
}();
pm.prototype.distanceToPoint = function () {
  const e = new ve();
  return function (n) {
    this.closestPointToPoint(n, e);
    return n.distanceTo(e);
  };
}();
pm.prototype.distanceToTriangle = function () {
  const e = new ve();
  const t = new ve();
  const n = ["a", "b", "c"];
  const i = new fm();
  const r = new fm();
  return function (o, a = null, l = null) {
    const u = a || l ? i : null;
    if (this.intersectsTriangle(o, u, !0)) {
      (a || l) && (a && u.getCenter(a), l && u.getCenter(l));
      return 0;
    }
    let d = 1 / 0;
    for (let h = 0; h < 3; h++) {
      let p;
      const A = n[h];
      const y = o[A];
      this.closestPointToPoint(y, e);
      p = y.distanceToSquared(e);
      p < d && (d = p, a && a.copy(e), l && l.copy(y));
      const E = this[A];
      o.closestPointToPoint(E, e);
      p = E.distanceToSquared(e);
      p < d && (d = p, a && a.copy(E), l && l.copy(e));
    }
    for (let h = 0; h < 3; h++) {
      const p = n[h];
      const A = n[(h + 1) % 3];
      i.set(this[p], this[A]);
      for (let y = 0; y < 3; y++) {
        const E = n[y];
        const b = n[(y + 1) % 3];
        r.set(o[E], o[b]);
        g9(i, r, e, t);
        const w = e.distanceToSquared(t);
        w < d && (d = w, a && a.copy(e), l && l.copy(t));
      }
    }
    return Math.sqrt(d);
  };
}();
var Gh = class {
  constructor(e, t, n) {
    this.isOrientedBox = !0;
    this.min = new ve();
    this.max = new ve();
    this.matrix = new Fn();
    this.invMatrix = new Fn();
    this.points = new Array(8).fill().map(() => new ve());
    this.satAxes = new Array(3).fill().map(() => new ve());
    this.satBounds = new Array(3).fill().map(() => new fy());
    this.alignedSatBounds = new Array(3).fill().map(() => new fy());
    this.needsUpdate = !1;
    e && this.min.copy(e);
    t && this.max.copy(t);
    n && this.matrix.copy(n);
  }
  set(e, t, n) {
    this.min.copy(e);
    this.max.copy(t);
    this.matrix.copy(n);
    this.needsUpdate = !0;
  }
  copy(e) {
    this.min.copy(e.min);
    this.max.copy(e.max);
    this.matrix.copy(e.matrix);
    this.needsUpdate = !0;
  }
};
Gh.prototype.update = function () {
  return function () {
    const t = this.matrix;
    const n = this.min;
    const i = this.max;
    const r = this.points;
    for (let u = 0; u <= 1; u++) {
      for (let d = 0; d <= 1; d++) {
        for (let h = 0; h <= 1; h++) {
          const p = r[1 * u | 2 * d | 4 * h];
          p.x = u ? i.x : n.x;
          p.y = d ? i.y : n.y;
          p.z = h ? i.z : n.z;
          p.applyMatrix4(t);
        }
      }
    }
    const s = this.satBounds;
    const o = this.satAxes;
    const a = r[0];
    for (let u = 0; u < 3; u++) {
      const d = o[u];
      const h = s[u];
      const p = r[1 << u];
      d.subVectors(a, p);
      h.setFromPoints(d, r);
    }

function k3e(e, t = null) {

    l = new Float32Array(s);

    u += s.byteLength;
  }
  function h(p, A, y = !1) {

    if (Bc(E, a)) {
      const b = ju(p, o);
      const w = oh(E, a);
      let T = 1 / 0;

      let R = 1 / 0;

      let O = -1 / 0;
      let z = -1 / 0;
      for (let Q = b, G = b + w; Q < G; Q++) {

        for (let F = 0; F < 3; F++) {

          const $ = r.getY(H);
          const te = r.getZ(H);

          $ > O && (O = $);
          te < R && (R = te);
          te > z && (z = te);
        }

      return l[p + 0] !== T || l[p + 1] !== x || l[p + 2] !== R || l[p + 3] !== D || l[p + 4] !== O || l[p + 5] !== z ? (l[p + 0] = T, l[p + 1] = x, l[p + 2] = R, l[p + 3] = D, l[p + 4] = O, l[p + 5] = z, !0) : !1;

    }

      if (!(t instanceof jN)) {
        throw new Error('MeshBVH: "intersectsTriangles" callback can only be used with another MeshBVH.');
      }
      const y = (E, b, w, T, x, R, D, O) => {
        for (let z = w, Q = w + T; z < Q; z++) {
          A(z);
          d.a.applyMatrix4(n);
          d.b.applyMatrix4(n);
          d.c.applyMatrix4(n);
          d.needsUpdate = !0;
          for (let G = E, N = E + b; G < N; G++) {
            u(G);
            o.needsUpdate = !0;
            if (s(o, d, G, z, x, R, D, O)) {
              return !0;
            }
          }
        }
        return !1;
      };
      if (r) {
        const E = r;
        r = function (b, w, T, x, R, D, O, z) {
          return E(b, w, T, x, R, D, O, z) ? !0 : y(b, w, T, x, R, D, O, z);
        };
      } else {
        r = y;
      }
    }
    return super.bvhcast(t, n, {
      intersectsRanges: r
    });
  }
  intersectsBox(t, n) {
    P3.set(t.min, t.max, n);
    P3.needsUpdate = !0;
    return this.shapecast({
      intersectsBounds: i => P3.intersectsBox(i),
      intersectsTriangle: i => P3.intersectsTriangle(i)
    });
  }
  intersectsSphere(t) {
    return this.shapecast({
      intersectsBounds: n => t.intersectsBox(n),
      intersectsTriangle: n => n.intersectsSphere(t)
    });
  }
  closestPointToGeometry(t, n, i = {}, r = {}, s = 0, o = 1 / 0) {
    return (this.indirect ? H3e : R3e)(this, t, n, i, r, s, o);
  }
  closestPointToPoint(t, n = {}, i = 0, r = 1 / 0) {
    return f3e(this, t, n, i, r);
  }
};

function aV(e) {
  if (uS === 0) {
    uS = e;
    return;
  }
  if (e - uS < X3e) {
    return;
  }
  const t = (e - uS) / 1e3;
  JN = [...Yx.entries()].map(([n, i]) => ({
    label: n,
    calls: Math.round(i.calls / t),
    ms: i.ms / t
  })).sort((n, i) => i.ms - n.ms);
  Yx = new Map();
  uS = e;
}
var If = {
  enabled: !1,
  count(e) {
    If.enabled && (aV(performance.now()), oV(e).calls++);
  },
  time(e, t) {
    if (!If.enabled) {
      return t();
    }
    const n = performance.now();
    try {
      return t();
    } finally {
      const i = performance.now();
      aV(i);
      const r = oV(e);
      r.calls++;
      r.ms += i - n;
    }
  },
  addToGauge(e, t) {
    H6.set(e, (H6.get(e) ?? 0) + t);
  },
  rows() {
    return JN;
  },
  gauges() {
    return [...H6.entries()];
  },
  reset() {
    Yx = new Map();
    JN = [];
    uS = 0;
  }
};

function nMe(e, t) {

  } of e) {

  }

function iMe(e, t) {
  e.updateMatrixWorld(!0);
  const {
    groups: n,
    skipped: i
  } = tMe(e, t);
  const r = e.matrixWorld.clone().invert();
  const s = [];
  const o = [];
  let a = 0;
  for (const u of n.values()) {
    if (u.length < Z3e) {
      a += u.length;
      continue;
    }
    const d = nMe(u, r);
    if (!d) {
      a += u.length;
      continue;
    }
    for (const {
      mesh: h
    } of u) {
      h.visible = !1;
      h.userData.batched = !0;
      o.push(h);
    }
    s.push(d);
  }
  const l = o.length;
  If.addToGauge("batch groups", s.length);
  If.addToGauge("batch meshes", l);
  If.addToGauge("batch skipped", i + a);
  return {
    nodes: s,
    groups: s.length,
    batched: l,
    skipped: i + a,
    saved: l - s.length,
    attach(u) {
      for (const d of s) {
        u.add(d);
        d.updateMatrix();
        d.updateMatrixWorld(!0);
      }
    },
    dispose() {
      for (const u of s) {
        u.removeFromParent();
        u.dispose();
      }
      for (const u of o) {
        u.visible = !0;
        delete u.userData.batched;
      }
      s.length = 0;
      o.length = 0;
    }
  };
}

function cie(e) {

var cV = 0;
var rMe = .08;

var oMe = 10.5;
var aMe = .16;
var lMe = .15;
var cMe = .9;
var e_ = {
  idle: 1,
  walk: 0,
  run: 0,
  sprint: 0

function mb(e, t, n, i) {
  e_.idle = e;
  e_.walk = t;
  e_.run = n;
  e_.sprint = i;

function uMe(e, t, n) {
  if (e <= z6) {
    return mb(1, 0, 0, 0);
  }
  if (t && e <= uV) {
    const i = (e - z6) / 2.5;
    return mb(1 - i, i, 0, 0);
  }
  if (e <= G6) {
    const i = t ? uV : z6;
    const r = (e - i) / (G6 - i);
    return t ? mb(0, 1 - r, r, 0) : mb(1 - r, 0, r, 0);
  }
  if (!n) {
    return mb(0, 0, 1, 0);
  }
  if (e <= oMe) {
    const i = (e - G6) / 3.5;
    return mb(0, 0, 1 - i, i);
  }
  return mb(0, 0, 0, 1);
}
function die(e) {
  const t = e.animations.find(re => re.name === "Idle");
  const n = e.animations.find(re => re.name === "Run");
  const i = e.animations.find(re => re.name === "Walk") ?? null;
  const r = e.animations.find(re => re.name === "Sprint") ?? null;
  if (!t) {
    throw new Error('player_model.glb is missing required "Idle" animation');
  }
  if (!n) {
    throw new Error('player_model.glb is missing required "Run" animation');
  }
  const s = cie(e.scene);
  s.traverse(re => {
    const ne = re;
    ne.isSkinnedMesh && (ne.frustumCulled = !1);
  });
  A9(s);
  const o = new UC(s);
  const a = o.clipAction(t);
  const l = o.clipAction(n);
  const u = i ? o.clipAction(i) : null;
  const d = r ? o.clipAction(r) : null;
  a.play();
  l.play();
  l.setEffectiveWeight(0);
  u && (u.play(), u.setEffectiveWeight(0));
  d && (d.play(), d.setEffectiveWeight(0));
  let h = 0;
  let p = 0;
  let A = 0;
  let y = 0;
  let E = 0;
  let b = [];
  let w = 0;
  let T = null;
  let x = null;
  let R = 1;
  let D = !1;
  let O = 0;
  function z(re) {
    const ne = b[re];
    const le = o.clipAction(ne.clip);
    le.reset();
    ne.times === 1 ? le.setLoop(lF, 1) : le.setLoop(Xw, ne.times);
    le.clampWhenFinished = Number.isFinite(ne.times);
    le.setEffectiveWeight(0);
    le.play();
    x = T;
    R = x ? 0 : 1;
    T = le;
    w = re;
  }
  function Q() {
    x && (x.stop(), x = null);
    T && (T.stop(), T = null);
    b = [];
    O = 0;
    R = 1;
  }
  function G(re) {
    const ne = re.flatMap(le => {
      const me = e.animations.find(Me => Me.name === le.clip);
      return me ? [{
        clip: me,
        times: Number.isFinite(le.times) ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(le.times)) : 1 / 0
      }] : (console.warn(`emotes: "${le.clip}" is not an animation in player_model.glb`), []);
    });
    ne.length !== 0 && (Q(), b = ne, z(0), D = !0);
  }
  o.addEventListener("finished", re => {
    !D || re.action !== T || (w + 1 < b.length ? z(w + 1) : D = !1);
  });
  function N(re, ne, le, me) {
    s.position.set(re, ne, le);
    s.rotation.y = me + cV;
    h = re;
    p = ne;
    A = le;
    y = me;
    E = 0;
  }
  function F(re, ne, le, me) {
    h = re;
    p = ne;
    A = le;
    y = me;
  }
  function H(re) {
    const ne = 1 - Math.exp(-re / rMe);
    const le = s.position;
    const me = le.x;
    const Me = le.z;
    le.x += (h - le.x) * ne;
    le.y += (p - le.y) * ne;
    le.z += (A - le.z) * ne;
    const Je = le.x - me;
    const ke = le.z - Me;
    const Z = re > 0 ? Math.hypot(Je, ke) / re : 0;
    const se = 1 - Math.exp(-re / sMe);
    E += (Z - E) * se;
    D && E > cMe && (D = !1);
    let X = y + cV - s.rotation.y;
    X = Math.atan2(Math.sin(X), Math.cos(X));
    s.rotation.y += X * ne;
  }
  function ee(re) {
    o.update(re);
    const ne = 1 - Math.exp(-re / aMe);
    O += ((D ? 1 : 0) - O) * ne;
    R < 1 && (R = Math.min(1, R + re / lMe), R >= 1 && x && (x.stop(), x = null));
    T && (T.setEffectiveWeight(O * R), x?.setEffectiveWeight(O * (1 - R)), !D && O < .01 && Q());
    const le = 1 - O;
    const me = uMe(E, u !== null, d !== null);
    a.setEffectiveWeight(me.idle * le);
    u?.setEffectiveWeight(me.walk * le);
    l.setEffectiveWeight(me.run * le);
    d?.setEffectiveWeight(me.sprint * le);
  }
  function $(re) {
    H(re);
    ee(re);
  }
  function te() {
    o.stopAllAction();
    o.uncacheRoot(s);
    s.parent?.remove(s);
  }
  return {
    group: s,
    snapTo: N,
    setTarget: F,
    playEmote: G,
    update: $,
    updateMotion: H,
    updateAnimation: ee,
    dispose: te
  };
}

function fMe(e, t) {
  const n = new Map();

  for (const r of e.children) {
    r.traverse(s => n.set(s.name, r.name));
    i.set(r.name, {
      node: r,
      clips: []
    });
  }
  for (const r of t) {
    const s = new Set(r.tracks.map(a => n.get(bo.parseTrackName(a.name).nodeName)));
    if (s.size !== 1) {
      continue;
    }
    const [o] = s;
    o !== void 0 && i.get(o)?.clips.push(r);
  }
  return i;
}

    }

    }

    }

var wMe = class {
  constructor(e = new ve(0, 0, 0), t = new ve(0, 1, 0), n = 1) {
